=== PROMPT ===
You will be given a competitive programming problem.
For a positive integer x, define f(x) as follows: "While x is even, keep dividing it by 2. The final value of x after these divisions is f(x)." For example, f(4)=f(2)=f(1)=1, and f(12)=f(6)=f(3)=3.
Given an integer sequence A=(A_1,A_2,\ldots,A_N) of length N, find \displaystyle \sum_{i=1}^N \sum_{j=i}^N f(A_i+A_j).

Input

The input is given in the following format from Standard Input:
N
A_1 A_2 \ldots A_N

Output

Print the answer.

Constraints


- 1\le N\le 2\times 10^5
- 1\le A_i\le 10^7
- All input values are integers.

Sample Input 1

2
4 8

Sample Output 1

5

f(A_1+A_1)=f(8)=1, f(A_1+A_2)=f(12)=3, f(A_2+A_2)=f(16)=1. Thus, Print 1+3+1=5.

Sample Input 2

3
51 44 63

Sample Output 2

384

Sample Input 3

8
577752 258461 183221 889769 278633 577212 392309 326001

Sample Output 3

20241214

Reason in a commit-implement-recheck way.

A productive rhythm for this task usually looks like this:
- Isolate the decisive constraints, the target complexity, and at most one or two approaches that are still truly plausible.
- If two candidates remain, compare them only on the decisive constraint and commit quickly to the stronger one instead of keeping a wide search open.
- Before coding, keep only a tiny implementation ledger for the chosen plan: main invariant, key state or transition, and the one or two edge cases most likely to fail.
- Move into code as soon as that ledger is clear. After coding starts, keep reasoning attached to the current draft rather than reopening the high-level search.
- After the first complete draft, do one short implementation-check-adjust-check loop on the same draft: test the key edge case, patch locally if needed, and recheck.
- Reopen the main design only if a concrete counterexample breaks the core invariant. Otherwise prefer the local patch and keep closing.
- Once the current draft, invariant, and short recheck still agree, finalize the solution.

Output only the final Python code enclosed in a code block.

Simplest:
import sys
def solve():
    data=list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    N=data[0]; A=data[1:]
    maxA=max(A)
    total=sum(A)
    ans = (N+1)*total  # U0? Wait formula ans = U0 - sum_{k>=1} U_k/2^k. So initialize ans = U0 = (N+1)*total. Then subtract for k>=1. But if we compute U_k for k>=1. Good.
    M=2
    k=1
    while M <= 2*maxA:
        # compute U_k
        cnt={}
        sm={}
        # maybe local variables for speed
        mask=M-1
        for a in A:
            r = a & mask
            cnt[r] = cnt.get(r,0)+1
            sm[r] = sm.get(r,0)+a
        ordered=0
        # iterate over items
        for r, cr in cnt.items():
            s = (-r) & mask  # since M power of two, (-r) mod M
            cs = cnt.get(s)
            if cs is not None:
                # contribution
                ordered += cs * sm[r] + cr * sm[s]
        # diag
        diag = 0
        # r=0 and r=M//2
        if 0 in sm:
            diag += sm[0]
        half = M//2
        if half in sm:
            diag += sm[half]
        diag *= 2
        U = (ordered + diag)//2

Now, maybe we can further optimize by precomputing A list and using local variables in loop. Code:

def solve():
    import sys
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data: return
    N = data[0]
    A = data[1:]
    total = sum(A)
    max_a = max(A)
    ans = (N + 1) * total
    M = 2
    limit = max_a * 2
    while M <= limit:
        mask = M - 1
        cnt = {}
        sm = {}
        get_cnt = cnt.get
        get_sm = sm.get
        for a in A:
            r = a & mask
            cnt[r] = get_cnt(r, 0) + 1
            sm[r] = get_sm(r, 0) + a
        ordered = 0
        # local refs
        for r, cr in cnt.items():
            s = (-r) & mask
            cs = cnt.get(s)
            if cs is not None:
                ordered += cs * sm[r] + cr * sm[s]
        diag = 0
        if 0 in sm:
            diag += sm[0]
        half = M >> 1
        if half in sm:
            diag += sm[half]
        diag <<= 1
        U = (ordered + diag) >> 1
        ans -= U // M
        M <<= 1
    print(ans)

Need be careful: `get_cnt = cnt.get` before updates; dict.get bound method remains valid after mutations? Yes, bound method to dict object, uses current dict. But if we assign `cnt[r] = ...`, get method still works. However, using `get_cnt` local and then updating cnt is fine. But `cnt.get(s)` inside inner loop after building; we can use `cnt_get = cnt.get` for speed. But if we set `cnt_get = cnt.get` before inner loop, okay. However, we also use `cnt.items()`. Good.

Let's write final code carefully:

import sys

def solve():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N = data[0]
    A = data[1:]
    total = sum(A)
    max_a = max(A)
    ans = (N + 1) * total

    M = 2
    limit = max_a * 2
    while M <= limit:
        mask = M - 1
        cnt = {}
        sm = {}
        cnt_get = cnt.get
        sm_get = sm.get
        for a in A:
            r = a & mask
            cnt[r] = cnt_get(r, 0) + 1
            sm[r] = sm_get(r, 0) + a

        ordered = 0
        cnt_get2 = cnt.get
        for r, cr in cnt.items():
            s = (-r) & mask
            cs = cnt_get2(s)
            if cs is not None:
                ordered += cs * sm[r] + cr * sm[s]

        diag = 0
        if 0 in sm:
            diag += sm[0]
        half = M >> 1
        if half in sm:
            diag += sm[half]
        diag <<= 1

        U = (ordered + diag) >> 1
        ans -= U // M
        M <<= 1

    print(ans)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    solve()

Now, let's consider if there is a possible issue with if the code's `M` loop for max_a=2246, limit=4492, M=4096 computed. Good.

Now, let's think about if there is a possible issue with if the code's `M` loop for max_a
</think>

=== ANSWER ===
```python
import sys

def solve():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return

    N = data[0]
    A = data[1:]

    total = sum(A)
    max_a = max(A)

    # U_0 = sum_{i<=j} (A_i + A_j) = (N+1) * sum(A)
    ans = (N + 1) * total

    M = 2
    limit = max_a * 2

    while M <= limit:
        mask = M - 1
        cnt = {}
        sm = {}

        cnt_get = cnt.get
        sm_get = sm.get

        for a in A:
            r = a & mask
            cnt[r] = cnt_get(r, 0) + 1
            sm[r] = sm_get(r, 0) + a

        # Sum over all ordered pairs (i, j) with A_i + A_j divisible by M
        ordered = 0
        for r, cr in cnt.items():
            s = (-r) & mask
            cs = cnt_get(s)
            if cs is not None:
                ordered += cs * sm[r] + cr * sm[s]

        # Diagonal pairs: 2 * A_i divisible by M
        diag = 0
        if 0 in sm:
            diag += sm[0]
        half = M >> 1
        if half in sm:
            diag += sm[half]
        diag <<= 1

        # U_k for k = log2(M)
        U = (ordered + diag) // 2

        # ans = U_0 - sum_{k>=1} U_k / 2^k
        ans -= U // M

        M <<= 1

    print(ans)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    solve()
```
